I'm not looking to get robbed.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person who frequently takes any other kind of pill, such as vitamins or illicit drugs.","Especially, someone with a frank substance use disorder who consumes a large amount of illicit pills, especially opioid street drug pills: synonym of pillhead."],"tags":["derogatory","informal","often"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":7637948,"upstream_offset":17440778466,"upstream_record_sha256":"6bb0f249a13015ffb9c8b0dd940cba209758cf67e67a71939604b89a166a6870","word":"pill popper"}
